Both had been reported in conversation and filed nowhere: *a decision that lives only in a conversation has exactly the durability of the conversation, and from the next person's position is indistinguishable from a decision never made.*
- **#312 strips the §10 enumeration** from `security-reviewer.md` and `architect.md` in favour of a pointer to ADR-011 Decision 4, which has read **three** (RT-22 / RT-26 / RT-27) since 2026-07-19 while both briefs still enumerated **two**. **Not cosmetic:** during the close-gate a database comment was corrected from `2` to `3`, and a reviewer cross-checking against their own brief rather than `DECISIONS.md` would have **reported the correct fix as the error** — confidently, because a role definition reads as authoritative in exactly the way a stale code comment does, *and it is consulted by the agent whose job is catching stale comments.* The standing read-Decision-4-verbatim rule is what prevented it, **paying for the first time that night, on its home surface, against its own reviewer.** A re-grep **after** editing found two more instances than were reported, including one in the `description:` frontmatter — *the sweep that missed them was run by the person best equipped to run it.*

Reported as a question rather than a finding, because RT-27 being catalogued does not imply it is fenced.

Until measured, both were *described* as `RT-22 / RT-26`, and **two coincidentally-equal descriptions are indistinguishable from one set**, while driving different triggers (ledger changes → joint-review-mandatory; fence-boundary changes → escalation).

That produced a silent instrument failure during review, where three `git show` calls returned nothing and read as *"these files contain no test plan at all"* rather than *"wrong directory"*, caught only by a positive control. The load-bearing clause is the second: *a binding is a claim about a ref — and so is a memory of having checked one*, so **re-derive-at-use applies to your own prior verification, not only to other people's claims.**

### v1.145 — 2026-08-04

**Phase 6 — ADR-042 Amendment 1 RATIFIED: `account_event` had no writer (PR #305, merge `2d6f1bc`).** Doc-only; merging the PR *was* the F/CTO ratification. **Nothing wrote `pfin.account_event`** — `058` creates five triggers and none writes it; repo-wide the table appeared outside `057` in exactly two places, both comments. As designed, `057` shipped an audit table, an INSERT RLS policy and the `#16` matched-tenant fence **for a write path nobody built**, so a closure would be gated and unrecorded. **The root cause is the transferable part:** Decision 5 specifies the audit writer as a trigger *distinct* from the gate, the build sequence collapses them into "the gate's trigger", and `058`'s own deliverables list never contains it — **so the writer appears in ADR-042 exactly once, as ordering rationale for a dependency, and never as a deliverable of any slice.** `058` was built from a complete enumeration; the missing item lived in another slice's reasoning. **A deliverable named only in another item's rationale is structurally unbuildable-from** — *consumer sweep when you CHANGE something; writer sweep when you CREATE something.* The absence is recorded as the finding rather than silently corrected, because a fixed build sequence with no note reads as though it was always right. A4 **replaces** Decision 5a's audit-column criterion with *exists at write time and cannot be reconstructed*; A5 specifies the writer as an AFTER trigger taking `auth.uid()` **first** with the GUC only when null, since `set local` is available to any session and **GUC-first is a forgeable system identity in an append-only record**; A6 carries PM's `reason_code` ruling. A7 leaves **one one-way door open and scheduled**: the vocabulary mixes *why the account ended* against *where the value went*, and the premise that the ledger recovers the second was **tested and withdrawn**. The irreversible thing is **column existence**, not requiredness and not the axis choice — a `disposition` column added later is NULL for every prior row, unbackfillable and append-only, so **it reads as reversible because the schema change is.** Cost ≈zero today, real at public signup; joins the consolidated pre-signup gate and does not block `057`.

Both defects share the shape the entry documents about itself: **every citation resolved, and the claim was still wrong.**
- **#299 / #300 / #301 work the §7.7 entries.** A new entry for a live defect where a **user-writable currency column silently restates historical net worth** — the event-recording half urgent independently of the rendering half, because the information needed to mark the affected range retroactively is destroyed by not recording it now, and **the window closes per user action, not per calendar day**. A timeline correction on an entry that would otherwise have **caused the defect it was written to prevent**, with the root cause recorded: *the obligations had been numbered, and three items in a numbered list read as three of the same kind — ordinals conceal heterogeneity.* And the **eighth checkpoint** — *after a ratify changes a mechanism, re-derive open findings rather than re-reading them; re-reading confirms a claim is well-formed, re-deriving confirms it is still true* — admitted over an earlier freeze at seven, because **that freeze was on a number while the stated stopping condition was a bar.** Its own limit ships with it, motivated by the uncomfortable case where two artifacts each state a correct number with different referents and **the act of reconciling them is the defect**.
- **#302 back-annotates ADR-013 A1–A3** with a forward reference to ADR-042, stating they are preserved and govern the **connection** column while ADR-042 retires a different column on a different table. The ruling was never missing — ADR-042 carried it explicitly — but **A1–A3 carried no forward reference at all, and every recorded conflation happened while reading A1 and PM-1, none while reading ADR-042.** The generalisable form: *put the disambiguation where the confusion happens, not where the decision was made.*

### v1.142 — 2026-08-03

**Phase 6 — ADR-042 ACCEPTED: account closure as a bookkeeping event (PR #297, merge `62804b5`).** Lands ADR-042 into `DECISIONS.md` **ahead of its migrations**, so they can cite it by anchor rather than by promise — the document had been authored, joint-reviewed and verified while living in a gitignored working directory, the exact exposure this review spent itself on. **A single boolean on the account table was carrying three unrelated concepts** — which aggregator an account is linked to, whether it is open or closed, and which incoming provider accounts to associate at connect time — and only one of those is an account-lifecycle fact. **Under double-entry, marking an account inactive while it still holds value is a bookkeeping error**: the value must be counter-booked, and flipping a flag does not do that. Nothing in the schema guarded the transition, so the ledger and the reported net worth could be put into silent disagreement through a normal UI action. The ratified model separates the three concepts; two were already handled elsewhere or needed no storage at all, and the remaining one becomes a **dated** event — which additionally fixes a temporal defect, since filtering a current-state flag into a past as-of date **retroactively rewrote history**. Records the standing zero-value invariant and the retirement of the boolean it replaces, and carries two prior-ADR amendments as amendments rather than implications. One constraint applies **before public signup** and is recorded in the ADR rather than left to discovery.

### v1.141 — 2026-08-03

**Phase 6 — SELF-214 `pfin.nav_daily` substrate + `pfin_etl` login role LANDED — Platform substrate 2 of 2 (PR #294, merge `23168f1`).** Completes the SELF-230→SELF-214 pair unblocking the V1.1 §2.1.2 NAV-over-time chain. Ships `pfin.nav_daily` (migration `054`) — one frozen NAV per `(users_id, nav_date)`, owner-only RLS on the direct `users_id = auth.uid()` anchor AND-ed with the inherited `025` aal2 step-up backstop, append-only cross-tier immutability fences (UPDATE/DELETE row-level + TRUNCATE statement-level, both INVOKER), NaN/±Infinity finiteness CHECK, and `UNIQUE(users_id, nav_date)`. **Forward-only** per `050`'s temporal constraint: `fn_compute_nav`'s active-only scope is sound only at `p_as_of = current_date`, so history must come from frozen checkpoints rather than on-the-fly recomputation. Plus the **W-1 impersonation worker** (`nav_daily.py`) — binds each tenant, `SET LOCAL ROLE authenticated` + synthetic claims to reuse the **locked INVOKER `fn_compute_nav`** under RLS (no new DEFINER, no second valuation implementation), tears down, then appends as `service_role`. Plus **migration `055`** — `pfin_etl`, a dedicated NOLOGIN + NOINHERIT login role (member of `service_role` + `authenticated`, no direct table grants, no password). QA pgTAP **`plan(63)` 63/63** with **21 isolated inversion runs** · ETL **118 passed / 16 skipped** · CI **14/14** incl. the authoritative `001→055` reset-stack run (Files=53, Tests=1000). **Governance FLAT: §10 = 3 · Decision-3 = 15 labeled / 12 DDL-realized (+0) · DEFINER allowlist = 4.** SECURITY gains **SD-24** (HIGH, `tenant-scoped-derivative`) + **RT-31** — *not* RT-28, which was already allocated at SELF-212. ADR-040 (substrate + W-1 worker model) and ADR-041 (the ETL login role) landed with it.

**Sec joint-review: GREEN unconditional, 10 findings (B1–B10) dispositioned; three F/CTO ratifies.** The two load-bearing outcomes. **B7 — write-tenant binding fence:** the table carried a SELECT policy only and the writer bypasses RLS, so nothing validated that a row's `users_id` was the tenant whose data was actually read — a worker bug could have frozen tenant A's net worth under tenant B's id, permanently, since the rows are append-only. `fn_nav_daily_assert_computed_for` (BEFORE INSERT, INVOKER) now asserts the row's tenant against a transaction-local GUC captured from **`auth.uid()` as the database resolved it**, never from the application's own variable — which additionally closes the path where a session-scoped legacy `request.jwt.claim.sub` could serve one tenant's NAV for every tenant with no code bug and no app-layer assertion failure. **B9 — a defect that had shipped green through four reviewers:** `INSERT … ON CONFLICT (users_id, nav_date) DO NOTHING` requires SELECT on the arbiter columns while the migration granted INSERT only; because the privilege check happens at planning time rather than on conflict detection, the cron would have raised `42501` on its **first** execution and written nothing, ever. Resolved with a **column-scoped grant** (`users_id`, `nav_date` — never `nav_value`), following the `026:196` precedent. Sec's reasoning for refusing a table-level grant is worth preserving: *precisely because `service_role` bypasses RLS, the ACL is the only remaining fence on that role*, so "RLS didn't constrain it, so the ACL needn't either" would justify granting SELECT on every table in `pfin` and dissolves `008`'s least-privilege model. **B1/B8 — the ETL login identity had never been decided:** the docs named `service_role`, which is `NOLOGIN` and can never be a direct-Postgres login identity, while the `.env.example` placeholder showed the `postgres` (schema-owner) connection form — an owner-class login would have inverted every fence in `054` (measured: as owner, `set session_replication_role='replica'` succeeds and an "immutable" row mutates). **B10:** the deploy step wrote the credential to the server log in cleartext under the measured `log_statement = ddl`; replaced with psql `\password` (client-side hashing, prompts, never enters `.psql_history`) while the role is still NOLOGIN, then a separate `ALTER ROLE … LOGIN`.

**Method note — one failure shape, seven surfaces: verifying a proxy for the thing rather than the thing.** Placeholder ≠ deployed identity · grant-absence ≠ table absence · unmeasured baseline before asserting a widening · mechanism purpose ≠ mechanism behaviour · `rolinherit` ≠ effective inheritance (PG 16+ makes inheritance **per-membership**, so `GRANT … WITH INHERIT TRUE` confers privileges while the flag still reads false) · `MEMBER` ≠ the right to `SET ROLE` (the mirror case: `WITH SET FALSE` leaves `MEMBER` true while the worker fails `42501` on every run) · committed tree ≠ working tree (`git diff --stat main..HEAD` compares **committed** trees and was blind to eight staged-then-modified files, including `054` itself). Every instance was caught, none shipped, and the mechanism was consistent: someone verified a claim rather than accepting it — usually a claim made by someone with more authority on that surface. The durable output is the verify-hooks now sitting where the next surface meets them: RT-31 legs (c)/(f)/(g)/(h), the corrected `git status --short` pre-commit gate, the false-fence principle (*an executable statement that enforces nothing is worse than a comment saying the same thing, because code implies enforcement*), and the forward-looking-vs-point-in-time distinction that scopes any future migration-comment sweep so it does not rewrite merged migrations' history.

**Follow-ups: 10 entries staged at `BACKLOG.md` §7.6**, parked there rather than Linear because the workspace is at its free-issue limit — a **booking** constraint, not a **capture** constraint (§7 is the ratified home per [ADR-017](DECISIONS.md#adr-017) Decision 2 and needs no Linear seat). Highest value is **S1**: a pgTAP assertion that `authenticated` holds no table-level SELECT on `plaid_items` / `linked_source` — the fence a comment in `008` falsely promised, converting ADR-023 C3 from an assertion into an enforced invariant. **S10 is a deploy blocker, not a merge blocker:** the assembled worker path has never run end to end. The SQL composition is proven (executed against a live database in a rolled-back transaction: NOINHERIT denies ambient privilege → impersonated read resolves the correct tenant → the B7 GUC survives teardown → the production statement inserts once and a re-run returns `row_count = 0`), but **authentication as `pfin_etl` and the Python layer above it are not** — and Backend's suite cannot reach them, because SQLite does not model Postgres grants.

INVOKER + RLS scope by `auth.uid()`) and `p_scope pfin.scope[]` (the `pfin.scope` type does not exist — `scope` is a free-text account label per ADR-004 Decision B; per-scope reporting is V2+ per PRD §2.1.7, additive-reversible) → final `(p_as_of date default current_date)` (mirrors the identical `049` R2/R3 findings). **(2) Debt-sign D-1:** liability leaf `current_market_value` + the liability group subtotal keep `049`'s natural-negative sign (debt reduces the running total), and `buildups.debt = −(liability subtotal)` = a positive magnitude so AC#4's `nav = gross_total − debt` reads literally and foots by natural summation (alt magnitude-everywhere rejected — forks the leaf sign from `049` and breaks the foot invariant). **(3) JSONB shape** exactly AC#1, `category` carries the asset/RE/liability discriminator (no extra key); **empty categories omitted** (a group appears only with ≥1 active account; buildups computed over the full active set regardless). **(4) Tax placeholders** `realized_tax_liab = 0::numeric` + `unrealized_tax_liab = 0::numeric` (Option A V1.1, Wave-1 precedent; documented V1.4 ramp). The AC#6 `inactive = FALSE` filter is **mechanical, not a design choice** — the real column is `is_active boolean` (`003`; no `inactive` column exists) and `049` already filters `where acc.is_active`, so composing on `049` inherits the current-state filter for free (no local predicate). The file carries a durable **RATIFY RECORD (A1–A5)** documenting each ratified decision as the post-ratify cross-check hook. **Sec joint-review GREEN-with-notes** (financial-calc + multi-tenant veto surface) — SECURITY INVOKER + `set search_path=''`, no writes, adds no SECURITY DEFINER entry; multi-tenant no-leak via INNER-join-on-PK (stronger than a LEFT join — 1:1, no multiplicity/drop), cross-tenant caller → `049` empty → `groups []` + coalesced-0 sums → `nav 0` (fails closed); A1–A5 record ↔ body verbatim, zero paraphrase/citation drift; governance ledger cross-checked against ADR-011 Decision 4 verbatim and FLAT. **QA** two-tenant pgTAP **32/32** (`supabase/tests/rls/051_fn_nav_composition_rls.sql`, `plan(32)`) — all 9 ratified invariants asserted, incl. the two load-bearing ones: **foot-to-NAV EXACT** (`nav == fn_compute_nav(as_of, true)`, `is()`, non-vacuous vs the all-accounts figure) and **two-tenant isolation** (B sees 0 of A's accounts, non-vacuous) + category grouping + subtotal/buildup math + is_active inheritance (inactive account in no leaf, not in buildups) + tax=0 + empty-category omission + unpriced→0-not-NaN + debt-sign D-1. Verified **non-destructively** (apply-in-txn + ROLLBACK; no `db reset` — F/CTO local data intact); authoritative gate = CI `pg_prove`. **CI 13/13 GREEN**; both paired artifacts (migration `051` + pgTAP battery) in the PR diff (660 insertions — no green-vacuous risk). **Governance FLAT: §10 catalogued 3 (RT-22/26/27) · Decision-3 15/12 (no new FK column) · SECURITY DEFINER allowlist 4 · RT-26 4.** Role-separated loop: Architect (`051` + design ratify) → F/CTO ratify → QA (`051` battery) + Sec (joint-review) → DevOps (CI + PR). Unblocked by SELF-224 (`049` substrate, v1.135) + SELF-322 (`050` fn_compute_nav is_active fix, v1.136). **Deferred follow-up (Sec, F/CTO-cleared):** the `groups_json` INNER join to `cat_order` + the `sums` `not in ('real_estate','liability')` partition hardcode the 7 canonical account_types (exhaustive today vs the `003` account_type CHECK, so unreachable); a future migration adding an 8th type to the CHECK without adding it to `cat_order` would foot that type into nav/buildups yet omit it from `groups[]` — a foot-vs-display maintenance coupling to address whenever account_type is next extended. **Next up: SELF-226** (§2.1.5 NAV-composition display UI — the frontend surface that binds to this JSONB tree). ### v1.136 — 2026-08-01

**Phase 6 — SELF-322 `fn_compute_nav` is_active scope fix LANDED — full-stack (PR #284, merge `cd08f62`).** Fixes a **live headline-NAV bug**: `pfin.fn_compute_nav` (`019`) filtered no `is_active`, so the §2.1.1 headline (`netWorth.ts` calls it directly for the NAV scalar) **over-counted value-bearing INACTIVE (soft-deleted / closed / sold) accounts** and disagreed with the §2.1.5 composition (`049`, active-only) — violating the `012` current-state convention. Surfaced by QA's foot-to-NAV parity test on SELF-224. **Fix = Option C (parameterize; ADR-039, F/CTO-ratified; A blanket / B compose-from-049 / D temporal-`deactivated_at` all rejected):** migration `050` CREATE-OR-REPLACEs `fn_compute_nav` as a **2-arg impl `(p_as_of date, p_active_only boolean)`** — `is_active`-gated on both legs when true (securities via LEFT JOIN to `pfin.account`; cash via `pfin.account`), **byte-identical to `019` when false** — plus a **1-arg wrapper `(p_as_of)`** delegating to `(p_as_of, false)` (CREATE OR REPLACE in place, NO DROP → `037` GL Unrealized memo's all-accounts dependency **untouched**). The §2.1.1 headline (`netWorth.ts`) switches to `(as_of, true)`. **No parameter default** (a default alongside the 1-arg wrapper → PG "function … is not unique" ambiguity; the wrapper supplies the false). **Temporal fence (load-bearing):** `p_active_only=true` is sound only at `p_as_of=current_date` (`is_active` is current-state, not temporal); the headline's `asOf` is `todayIso()` = current_date (Backend-verified at the sole call site) — never historical+true; a future §2.1.2 trajectory / `nav_daily` must freeze active-only per precomputed checkpoint. **Tightens ADR-038's foot-to-NAV invariant to EXACT** (`Σ 049.current_market_value(active) = fn_compute_nav(as_of, true)`). **Posture FLAT: SECURITY INVOKER both fns (DEFINER allowlist 4) · §10 catalogued ledger 3 (RT-22/26/27) · Decision-3 15/12 (no new FK column).** **Sec joint-review GREEN** — byte-identity diffed `019`↔`050`; LEFT-JOIN no-leak proven (attaches account data to existing holdings, never adds foreign rows); cross-tenant → 0 on both signatures. **QA** two-tenant pgTAP **23/23** (`supabase/tests/rls/050_fn_compute_nav_active_only_rls.sql`) incl. the **§2.1.1 == §2.1.5 Consistency AC** (`Σ 049 active == fn_compute_nav(as_of, true) == 6500`), the `037`-memo regression (all-accounts NAV unchanged via the 1-arg), cross-tenant fail-closed both sigs, unpriced-asset → 0-not-NaN, liability R-7 sign. Backend `netWorth.test.ts` 9/9. CI all green (incl. web-app build). **Unblocks SELF-225** (§2.1.5 NAV-composition backend — next up). Non-blocking doc-nit noted in ADR-039: `046`'s "netWorth.ts filters is_active" comment refers to the account-existence *count* query, not the NAV *scalar*. SELF-322 Done.

### v1.135 — 2026-08-01

**Phase 6 — SELF-224 §2.1.5.a per-account unrealized-G/L primitive LANDED — first V1.1 "Net worth full" build feature (PR #282, merge `80de1b2`).** New SECURITY INVOKER read primitive `pfin.fn_account_unrealized_gl(p_as_of date)` (migration `049`) returning, per non-inactive account, `(account_id, current_market_value, cost_basis, unrealized_gl)` — feeds the §2.1.5 NAV-composition table (SELF-225/226). **Design (ADR-038, F/CTO-ratified):** **(1) Cost-basis path = GL-derived carried book** — securities `cost_basis` = Σ(`fn_gl_entries` `trade_position` × fx→USD), the single basis truth (== GL == tax == monthly reports); rejected lot-level-direct (re-implements `037`'s sell-removal → drift; depends on `lot_match` populated) and avg-cost (competing method). Lot-split forward path confirmed **additive** (a future per-lot helper reconciles to this primitive's per-account totals by construction; lot-level UI is V2). **(2) Market-value scope = Design C (account-total, symmetric)** — `current_market_value` = securities MV + roll-forward cash (uniform, all types) → **foots to per-account NAV** (SELF-225 sums leaf values to the NAV top-line); investment `cost_basis` = securities book + the SAME cash term (cancels), `unrealized_gl` = securities MV − securities book. Codifies the **scope-matching principle**: `gl = mv − cost_basis` is correct only when mv and cost_basis share scope. Rejected securities-only (A — understates NAV by investment-account cash) and mixed-scope (B — phantom gain). `cost_basis` is redefined to account-total book (internal — SELF-225/226 consume only `current_market_value` + `unrealized_gl`; no consumer reads `cost_basis`). **(3) Signature corrected** from the schema-impossible PM draft: `account_id bigint` (not UUID), dropped `p_users_id` (INVOKER+RLS scope by `auth.uid()` — confused-deputy foot-gun), dropped `p_scope` (no `pfin.scope` type — `scope` is a free-text label per ADR-004 Decision B), USD-fx normalization; 4-column contract (`account_type` discriminator declined — NULL-vs-zero on `cost_basis` encodes the investment branch). **Posture FLAT: SECURITY INVOKER (DEFINER allowlist 4) · §10 catalogued ledger 3 (RT-22/26/27) · Decision-3 15/12 (no new FK column).** **Sec joint-review GREEN** — financial-calc + multi-tenant veto surface; cross-tenant fail-closed confirmed load-bearing (INVOKER over the `003` `account_select` direct-owner RLS; every value CTE LEFT-JOINs onto the RLS-filtered account). **QA** two-tenant pgTAP battery **28/28** (`supabase/tests/rls/049_fn_account_unrealized_gl_rls.sql`) incl. the **foot-to-NAV parity assertion** (Σ current_market_value = fn_compute_nav over active accounts) + **AC#6 perf 33–40ms/20-acct (~8× under the 300ms budget — conditional-lock fallback not triggered)**. CI 13/13 green; both paired artifacts (migration + battery) + ADR-038 committed together. **Follow-up spawned — SELF-322:** QA's foot-to-NAV test surfaced a pre-existing `fn_compute_nav` (`019`) `is_active` scope gap — it counts inactive accounts while `049` correctly excludes them (PRD §2.4.2 current-state) → the §2.1.1 headline NAV and §2.1.5 composition NAV would disagree on a value-bearing inactive account; tracked as SELF-322 (V1.1, blocks SELF-225). SELF-224 Done.

All-frontend + read-only RLS loader + dev-script — **no migration, no new RLS surface, no Sec joint-review triggers.** All 14 CI checks green; svelte-check 0/0; browser-driven vs local Supabase (Hub renders grouped accounts + chips; unauth → 303 `/login` was 404; nav + rename + provider label + help-text verified). ### v1.123 — 2026-07-30

**Phase 6 — SELF-206 §2.4.4.a Plaid webhook handler LANDED — full-stack (PR #261, merge `f2268ed`); ADR-037. + doc-sync (meta/doc PR).** The **third and final V1.0 §2.4 connection feature**, built end-to-end through the full role-separated loop with **two design-time capability-verify catches** that reshaped the build before any code shipped. **(1) RT-05 = asymmetric ES256/JWK, not HMAC:** `plaid@27` webhook verification is a `Plaid-Verification` ES256 JWT verified against a PUBLIC EC/P-256 JWK fetched by `kid` from `/webhook_verification_key/get` — the June AC's static `PLAID_WEBHOOK_SECRET` HMAC primitive does not exist. **Option 1 (F/CTO-ratified):** the credentialed JWK *fetch* is delegated to a new provider-sync worker admission route (the sole holder of `PLAID_CLIENT_ID/SECRET`); `api/src` verifies the ES256 JWT + `request_body_sha256` raw-body-hash locally with `jose@^6.2.5` (ES256-pinned, `iat`-bounded) → `api/src` stays credential-less. **(2) No interactive transaction over PostgREST:** ARCH §3.1's single `BEGIN SERIALIZABLE` is not expressible from supabase-js, so the atomic write body moved into **Route Z-INVOKER** (migration `045`): `fn_plaid_webhook_resolve` (read-only tenant-resolve + idempotency pre-check) + `fn_plaid_webhook_commit` (atomic `connection_status` flip + `linked_source_state_history` + `linked_source_sync_audit` gate), **both SECURITY INVOKER** (service_role caller → no DEFINER bump) + the ratified **OWD-A A3 typed `sync_cursor` column**. **C-X2 lost-sync closure = sync-first / gate-at-completion (Close-i, F/CTO-locked):** the gate row is written only after the worker sync confirms, so a crash mid-flight is re-driven by Plaid's at-least-once retry + sync idempotency — self-contained, no poll dependency (a code-level Plaid poll exists via `poll.ts`/SELF-279; operational cadence = SELF-213/DevOps). **Per-path idempotency (money-flow correctness):** TRANSACTIONS events carry `provider_event_id = NULL` and dedup via the `/transactions/sync` cursor + `fn_ingest_transactions ON CONFLICT` (a content-hash key false-collides on Plaid's byte-identical same-second `SYNC_UPDATES_AVAILABLE` bodies and would silently drop a legit sync — QA **inversion-proved** the over-dedup guard); STATE/ITEM events carry a per-delivery `provider_event_id` gate. **Two new worker admission routes** (`/admission/webhook-verification-key` + `/admission/sync`) on the EXISTING RT-27 listener → routes-on-RT-27, §10 stays 3. **Sec joint-review GREEN + 4 mods** (RT-05 per-path replay-defense doc split; a foreign-Item ack-drop forensic WARN; a **docstring reconciliation catching a latent C-X2 regression vector** — two docstrings still described an abandoned commit-then-kick ordering + a false poll-self-heal; and **Decision-3 #15 enumeration fold-in** reconciling canonical ADR-011 D3 14/12 → **15/13** to the already-shipped SELF-207/`044` fence). **Ledgers flat: §10 3 · Decision-3 15/13 · SECURITY DEFINER 4 · RT-26 4.** **QA:** pgTAP `plan(35)` (the inversion-proven over-dedup guard + two-tenant isolation + cursor ACL) + a 9-case RT-05 ES256 adversarial battery; combined tree api check 0/0 + vitest **386** / worker typecheck + vitest **280** / pgTAP **750**; all CI fences green (RT-26 allowlist stays 4). **DevOps:** dead `PLAID_WEBHOOK_SECRET` removed (`.env.example` / `secrets-manifest.yml` / `deployment-runbook.md`; non-overlap fence re-verified) + a new `api/` `jose` dependency-vuln-audit CI job. **Doc-sync (this meta PR):** ADR-037 AC1 cell amended to the ES256/JWK model; SECURITY §4.5 RT-05 rewritten + RT-27 new-routes annotation; ARCH §3.1 retargeted to Route Z-INVOKER + the generic `linked_source` substrate (mermaid sequence rewrite, browser-verified). ### v1.111 — 2026-07-26

**Phase 6 — SELF-202 manual cash-transaction entry LANDED — the FIRST Onboarding §2.4 issue toward closing V1.0 (PR #240, merge `ff61a23`).** Full-stack §2.4.3.a (form UI + backend write path + edit + split), delivered through the complete role-separated build loop.

**The append-only re-derivation (ADR-032).** The 2026-06-03 AC assumed mutable `skip_flag`/`split_flag` + in-place edit — all rejected by the `004` immutability lock. F/CTO ratified re-deriving to the append-only model and, critically, **eliminating "skip" entirely**: a "skip" is a report-view filter, not stored data. A transfer is a real event → categorize it `Transfer` (the `028` taxonomy; the GL already Σ=0-offsets it via `group_type='transfer'`); "don't show category X in report Y" is a query/UI `WHERE` filter. ADR-032 generalizes the principle (**derive-don't-store**: split-double-count and transfer-hiding are both derived reader rules, not stored flags). The `skip ≠ reverse` distinction is the ground: a reversed txn *didn't happen* (leaves NAV/balance); there is no "happened-but-hidden" stored state.

**Migration `038` + ADR-032** (Sec-GREEN). PART A un-dorms `pfin.account_trans_split` writes — 3 `wr_access`-JOIN policies (INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE) mirroring `023`'s `ata_*`, each aal2-claused, + grant; no new table/column/fn (the Σ=parent deferred trigger, the `#13` matched-tenant fence, and the T-account view already exist in `029` — un-dorming *activates* them). PART B adds `fn_create_manual_trans` — a SECURITY INVOKER write-composition RPC (atomic `account_trans` INSERT + conditional `023` category annotation, under the caller's RLS). **Fact edit** = `004` reverse-and-replace (INSERT reversal + corrected row; the immutable ledger is never mutated); **split** = the un-dormed `029` path under the locked child-lifecycle rule (per parent, commit-surviving states are {0 children → count parent} XOR {N children Σ=parent → count children}; partial/unbalanced rejected at COMMIT).

**Folded-in security fix (SELF-304).** `038` also ALTERs `account_trans_split_select` to add the aal2 backstop conjunct — `029` shipped its SELECT un-claused because it post-dated the `025` retrofit, leaving a live C3 gap (aal1-readable split amounts for a totp-declared user). Aal2 clause byte-verified verbatim against `025`. Filed + closed as SELF-304.

**Backend / Frontend.** Backend: 5 SvelteKit actions (`createTrans` / `editTransFact` / `recategorize` / `splitTrans` / `unsplitTrans`) + Zod `.strict()` (server = security boundary) + reverse-and-replace + split create/replace/unsplit helpers; capability-verify clean-apply smoke PASS under the real `authenticated` role. Frontend: entry form (Money in/out toggle **derives the sign** — the user never types −), split editor (live running-sum, submit blocked until balanced + ≥2 lines), transaction row (edit/categorize/split/unsplit), account-detail page; tokens-only, no skip UI.

**Gate.** Sec joint-review **GREEN ×2** — migration DDL (aal2 clause fidelity, C3 fix, wr_access chains, RPC composition, ledgers flat-verified-live) + app-layer money-flow (reverse-and-replace netting, split-REPLACE money-safety, `.strict()` mass-assignment, tenant-binding — no service_role/cross-tenant). QA `plan(21)` two-tenant + inversion-proven, plus the required `029` BLOCK-4 removal (paired — directory-mode CI). CI fully green (pgTAP battery + Live-DB + web build + fences); `npm run check` 0; vitest 226 (+13). **Ledgers flat:** §10 = 3 · DEFINER allowlist 4/3 (RPC is INVOKER) · Decision-3 unchanged (no new FK; `#13` activated, `#10` exercised). 16 files, +2748/−75. SELF-202 + SELF-304 Done.

**Context around the landing.** A milestone-rotation check (2026-07-26) confirmed **no rotation is due** — the Double-Entry GL is a Linear *project*, not a V1.x milestone, and V1.0 is not closed. A V1.0 reconciliation flipped three stale-opens to Done (SELF-210 NAV backend superseded by `fn_compute_nav`; SELF-191 Plaid SDK scaffold; SELF-182 CI pipeline — all shipped but never updated in Linear), leaving the genuine V1.0 gap concentrated in **Onboarding §2.4** (SELF-199/200/203/204/205/206/207/208 + the SELF-209 close-gate + SELF-184). The GL follow-ups SELF-302/303 were re-homed to the V1.4 tax cluster; 5 SELF-202 fast-follows filed (SELF-305–309: 2 Architect hardenings, the exact-id embed, 2 designer polish). **NEXT: continue Onboarding §2.4 (SELF-203/204/200/209 + co-root 199) to close V1.0.**

### v1.110 — 2026-07-25

**Phase 6 — SELF-298 cross-tenant fence-message softening LANDED — the Double-Entry GL project's non-blocking cleanup fast-follow (PR #237, merge `1007ec9`).** A carried backlog item from the SELF-295 (M2) and SELF-296 (M3-basis) Sec joint-reviews, cleared now that the GL project core is complete.

**The defect it fixes.** Four SECURITY INVOKER matched-tenant fences — #10 (`023` sub_cat), #12 (`033` journal), the `030` Trade-constraint read, and the `034` basis_adjust reason read — sit behind the `025` aal2 step-up backstop. An own-tenant `aal1` + `mfa_policy='totp'` caller reading their OWN row hits the aal2-gated RLS, the fence's `NOT EXISTS` read returns empty, and it RAISEd a "cross-tenant … rejected" message. Fail-closed and safe, but the attribution is wrong — the cause is "not visible under the current AAL", not cross-tenant.

**Migration message rewordings** (message-text only — no fence-logic change, no new migration): #10 and #12 now read "… — not found, not visible under current AAL, or cross-tenant (ADR-011 Decision 3 canonical instance #NN …)"; the `030`/`034` "cannot resolve fact … fail-closed" reads append "— not found or not visible under current AAL". All four keep their ADR-011 Decision 3 instance-# citations. Fences #5 (`012`) / #8 (`022`) / `029` share the string but are **not** aal2-gated, so they were deliberately left untouched.

**Test batteries — decouple from message text.** The paired assertions on the reworded messages were switched from `throws_like('%msg%')` to `throws_ok(…, 'P0001', null, …)` — P0001 specifically (not bare, not 42501) so the assertion still distinguishes a fence RAISE from an RLS denial, preserving the "004 all-42501 false-green" teeth. 12 assertions flipped: `033` 3a/3b/3c/3d/3e/3h, `030` 7a/7b, `034` B12, and `023` F1/F2/F3. Business-rule RAISEs (consistency / sign-alignment / domain / reason↔amount) kept message-precise `throws_like`.

**Brief-drift catch.** The Linear AC enumerated only the `033`/`030`/`034` batteries, but rewording #10 also breaks the `023` battery (F1/F2/F3 assert the #10 message) — those would have hard-RED'd CI. Caught during scoping and flipped.

**Gate — Sec + QA joint-review (D3 cross-tenant FK-bypass family surface), AMBER → GREEN.** Both reviewers independently caught the same lone defect: `033_journal_rls.sql:315` (3e) had its args swapped to the `throws_ok` 4-arg shape but the function name left as `throws_like` — a malformed call that would abort the file's transaction. Fixed. Sec confirmed all four fences remain fail-closed (message-only edit, no `(subquery) <> …` NULL-leak), no new information disclosure, and the ledgers flat; QA confirmed no assertion count lost, the AC-missed `023` set caught, and the business-rule `throws_like` correctly preserved. **§10 = 3 · SECURITY DEFINER allowlist = 4 · Decision-3 = 14 labeled / 12 DDL-realized — all unchanged, zero ledger movement.**

**Verification.** Local `supabase test db` (fresh build of `001`–`037`): Files=34, Tests=575, PASS. CI green — pgTAP RLS battery + RT-22/RT-26/RT-27 + TenantBoundConnection greps + secrets/dep scans. SELF-298 Done. **The Double-Entry GL project is fully closed — no GL feature work remains; NEXT is the V1 feature clusters (§2.1.2+ net-worth / §2.2 allocation / §2.3 cash-flow / §2.5 taxes / §2.6 monthly-report) or a milestone-rotation check.**

### v1.109 — 2026-07-25

**Phase 6 — `037` GL-completion LANDED — the tenth-and-LAST Double-Entry GL migration; CLOSES the GL project core (SELF-301; PR #234, merge `72b88f9`).** The write-side completion over the now-writable `lot_match` (`036`). Authored across a design-then-fix arc: an initial 4-component author pass, then a Sec-gated F1 fix, then an F/CTO-ratified compound-close hardening (both QA and the Architect independently found the ratified compound Σ=0 check vacuous).

**Migration `037`** (single file, 4 components):
1. **`fn_gl_entries` create-or-replace** — the matched-**SELL realized-gain** branch (F/CTO-ratified **Option A**): Dr Cash / Cr Position(current carried book) / one Realized-Gain line to equity; `matched_book` = FIFO acquisition + pro-rata position-level basis_adjust; **balanced-by-construction** (3 legs sum to 0). ST/LT + depreciation-recapture **character is derived in the tax layer** (`fn_compute_tax_liability`) from the lot_match + basis_adjust history — NOT posted into the trial balance. Unmatched sells keep `035`'s Suspense-park floor (`not exists lot_match` guard); corp_action/wash_sale unchanged (Suspense floor).
2. **`fn_journal_close_balance`** — INVOKER BEFORE UPDATE on `pfin.journal` open→closed: a top-of-close **future-dated-leg reject (ALL group_types)** → group_type branch (`transfer`→Σ(amount)=0; `transfer_in_kind`→per-security Σ(qty)=0; **`compound`→no-Suspense-residual gate**) → write-once `close_book_value`+`closed_at`; closed→open nulls both.
3. **`pfin.journal` ALTER** — `close_book_value` + `closed_at` (the write-once close snapshot).
4. **`fn_account_trans_annotation_freeze_closed`** — INVOKER BEFORE INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE on the `023` overlay; rejects attach/detach **AND reclassification** of a leg whose OLD-or-NEW journal is `status='closed'` (F/CTO-ratified stronger form — a post-close reclass would otherwise drift a compound group's re-derived Σ; reopen unfreezes).

Plus **F1** (Sec joint-review): `journal_insert` WITH CHECK gains `and status = 'open'` (reject a born-`'closed'` insert that would bypass the conservation check + snapshot). The Architect did a full **append-only re-create** of `journal_insert` reproducing `033`'s WITH CHECK **including the `025` aal2 backstop verbatim** (Sec byte-verified); `033`'s file untouched.

**F/CTO ratifications this migration** (surfaced one-at-a-time): SELL realized-gain **Option A** (one line to equity vs split recapture/capital) · Σ=0-close **Option B** (group_type-branched) · close-snapshot **Option A + membership-freeze guard** · **defer** wash_sale + substantive corp_action (Suspense floor) · **F1 gate-now** · the **compound-close hardening** (no-Suspense-residual + reject-future-dated, replacing the vacuous literal total-Σ) · **hoist** the future-dated reject to all group_types · the **stronger freeze** (membership + classification).

**The compound-vacuity finding.** The ratified compound branch (`sum(amount_book) over journal_id`) is **vacuous by construction** — every leg's postings self-balance within their own `journal_id` (real leg + imputed contra), so per-group Σ is always 0 and the check never fires (a lone unclassified −$100 leg images to Cash −100 + Suspense +100 → "balances" → closes, unresolved). Both QA and the Architect surfaced it independently; F/CTO ratified the meaningful equivalent: **block the close if any leg routes to Suspense** (`entry_class = 'suspense'`) — the true "group fully resolved" gate, fail-closed, un-bypassable (Suspense is derived, not user-settable), and — unlike the vacuous version — **non-vacuously testable**.

**Ledgers FLAT — the GL project closes at ZERO movement: §10 = 3** (RT-22 + RT-26 + RT-27; authenticated-tier INVOKER + 2 plain columns) **· SECURITY DEFINER allowlist = 4** (both new triggers INVOKER, `search_path=''`) **· Decision-3 = 14 labeled / 12 DDL-realized, unchanged** (no new FK — the freeze guard READS the existing `023.journal_id` (#12) target status; the snapshot is 2 plain columns). Decision 4 read verbatim, Path B, 3-axis clean.

**Gate — Sec joint-review-mandatory (money-flow + financial-calc + close-enforcement + freeze guard), AMBER → FULL GREEN.** Sec GREEN'd the flows/ledgers on the first pass (balanced-by-construction confirmed; the stronger freeze guard blessed; cross-tenant isolation holds) and held AMBER on **F1** (the insert-closed invariant hole — bounded blast radius since the freeze guard means such a journal is empty, but violates *closed ⟹ closed_at written*) + **B1** (missing paired battery). Both discharged: F1 fixed (aal2 byte-verified), the compound-vacuity hardened, and the `plan(26)` battery finalized — Sec flipped to **FULL GREEN**, verifying `033`'s (6c) totp+aal1 assertion is a live regression guard on the **037-recreated** `journal_insert` policy (the CI reset stack is cumulative). QA `plan(26)` two-tenant, inversion-proven, **Finding-2 resolved** (the compound branch is now a real, reachable fence). The `035` battery gained a paired fixture-fix (a `035` fixture attached a leg to a journal that `037`'s freeze trigger now closes → leg re-pointed to an open journal; `plan(13)` unchanged). Backend clean-apply `001`→`037` under PG17: full suite **Files=34 Tests=575 PASS** (`037` 26/26 · `035` 13/13 · `033` 28/28); no regression, no fixture collision.

**Phase 6 — M4-GL-read (`fn_gl_entries`) LANDED — the eighth Double-Entry GL migration + the M4-GL READ surface (SELF-297; PR #230, merge `7d001c3`).** M4-GL is the derived GL/trial-balance read layer over the immutable `account_trans` ledger. **Migration `035`** — `pfin.fn_gl_entries(p_as_of date) RETURNS TABLE(users_id, as_of, source_trans_id, split_id, journal_id, account_id, entry_account, security_id, entry_class, side, currency, amount_book)`: a single **SECURITY INVOKER** function, `set search_path=''`, `revoke execute from public` + `grant execute to authenticated` (no `service_role`, no `anon`) — an exact posture-clone of the only landed Lock-11 helper `fn_compute_nav`@`019`. Read-only over existing tables (no base table, no writes, no new DEFINER). Returns **flat GL entry rows** (Option A — a trial-balance aggregate is a derivable wrapper, never the base).

**Design property (load-bearing): correct-by-construction balance.** Every source fact emits book-value postings summing to exactly 0 (real leg + imputed/derived contra), so `SUM(amount_book)=0` holds **globally AND per-currency by construction**; misclassification only routes a contra to the derived **Suspense** line — it never unbalances. 11 imputation branches (cash-by-class Revenue/Expense/Equity/Transfer→Journal-Clearing-if-grouped, splits per-child, acct_setup→Opening-Balance-Equity, BUY→Dr Position/Cr Cash, basis_adjust depreciation→Dr Depreciation-Expense, return_of_capital→cash+basis, …). Unrealized-Gains is a memo pair (`fn_compute_nav − book NAV`, USD-balanced). The `fn_compute_nav` sub-call is itself INVOKER → INVOKER-under-INVOKER, no DEFINER RLS-bypass anywhere in the composition.

**`036`-deferral list (carried forward in the `035` CONTRACT block, mirroring `032`/`033`→"M4-GL PR", now →`036`):** (1) security SELL realized-gain / position-basis-removal — lot-match-dependent; `035` parks proceeds Dr Cash / Cr Suspense; **recapture** = `min(gain, accum_depreciation)` authored-ready but vacuous until `036` populates `lot_match`; (2) corp_action GL → Suspense; (3) basis_adjust wash_sale P&L → Suspense; (4) `Σ=0`-at-close enforcement trigger on `pfin.journal` status→'closed' (`033` B-ii); (5) lot_match INSERT grant + `wr_access` WITH CHECK policy (`032`) — **activates Decision-3 #14** (Sec N1: the #14 *fence* is already DDL-realized at `032`; `036` activates the write grant/policy that makes it exercisable); (6) `closed_at`/close-snapshot storage column (`033` B-ii).

Decision 4 read verbatim; Path B (references, does not restate the numbered ledger).

**Gate — Sec routine review (NOT joint-review-mandatory; read-only surface over money data), VERDICT GREEN.** All 9 checklist items confirmed: INVOKER posture, `search_path=''`, grant hygiene, cross-tenant isolation fails-closed, §10-stays-3, DEFINER-unchanged, Decision-3-unchanged, **zero write surface leaked** (whole-file scan: one `create or replace function` + `revoke`/`grant`/`comment`; no INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE grants, no policies, no triggers, no ALTERs), and the 6-item `036`-deferral list accurate. ### v1.106 — 2026-07-24

**Phase 6 — M3-basis (basis_adjust row-shape constraints) LANDED — the seventh Double-Entry GL migration (SELF-296; PR #228, merge `02d5d90`).** A `basis_adjust` is a dated BOOK adjustment to a security's cost basis (depreciation / return-of-capital / wash-sale) — magnitude rides `cost_basis`, not `amount`. The `basis_adjust` transaction_type value + the `metadata` jsonb column both already landed at `030`, so `034` adds only the row-SHAPE constraints; the basis roll-forward / accumulated-depreciation / recapture COMPUTE at M4-GL (deferred, like M2's `Σ=0`).

**Migration `034`** — (1) single-table CHECK `account_trans_basis_adjust_shape`: `transaction_type <> 'basis_adjust' OR (quantity=0 AND security_id IS NOT NULL AND cost_basis IS NOT NULL)` — reason-independent, role-agnostic (fires under the `017` service_role ingest), idempotent. (2) cross-table SECURITY INVOKER trigger `fn_account_trans_annotation_basis_adjust_reason` on `account_trans_annotation` (BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE; `search_path=''`; NULL-safe fail-closed; `revoke execute from public`; a structural clone of `030`'s Trade-constraint trigger): **R1** domain (`metadata.reason ∈ {depreciation, return_of_capital, wash_sale}`), **R2** reason↔amount (`return_of_capital ⟺ amount≠0`, anchored to the frozen `004` amount), **R3** additive guard (`reason` present ⟹ `basis_adjust`). (3) a **reserved-metadata-keys registry** on the `023.metadata` comment (`reason`=basis_adjust-only, `action`=corp_action-only) closing the discoverability gap the collision exposed.

**F/CTO-ratified (2026-07-24): A-i** (`reason` on the mutable `023` overlay per ADR-031 Amendment 1 item 8 — the immutable `cost_basis`-delta + `amount` facts determine the economics; `reason` is the interpretive label) · **B-i** (CHECK + overlay trigger) · **C** (`cost_basis` role-widens acquisition→signed delta; no `017` constraint to relax — verified only the NaN-fence, and the one-directional `qty_requires_security` CHECK doesn't collide) · **D** (roll-forward / accumulated-depreciation / recapture deferred to M4-GL) · **KEEP R3** (`metadata.reason` is a RESERVED basis_adjust-only key — reversibility-asymmetry: reserve while empty, relax later free; Architect + Security both concurred).

**Ledgers: §10 3 (unchanged) · SECURITY DEFINER allowlist 4 (zero new — CHECK + INVOKER trigger; `pg_catalog`-verified 3 authored) · Decision-3 14/12 unchanged** (value/tenancy-neutral; no FK-shaped column). `004` immutability unweakened (the CHECK is INSERT-time DDL, not the UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE triple-fence).

**Gate — Sec review (A-i was a Sec-consult on immutability semantics), CONDITIONAL-GREEN → FINAL GREEN.** Sec confirmed `004` unweakened + INVOKER/no-new-DEFINER + ledgers flat; ruled **R3 KEEP** (financial-correctness — a stray `metadata.reason` on a non-basis_adjust row would poison M4-GL's reason-keyed depreciation aggregation); ruled the **aal2-gated-INVOKER-read edge** benign (fail-closed, honest message) → folds into SELF-298. QA `plan(19)` 19/19, **inversion-proven** (two surgical mutants: CHECK→always-true reds only A3/A4/A5; R2→false reds only B3/B7/B11).

**The 030/031 collision (caught by the full-suite gate + resolved).** `034` clean-applied and its own battery passed 19/19 in isolation, but Backend's authoritative directory-mode full-suite run FAILED — `034` regressed the pre-existing `030` & `031` pgTAP fixtures: the CHECK rejected `030`'s bare-basis_adjust vocab-test row, and R3 rejected two fixtures using `metadata.reason` as a generic jsonb payload on `standard` rows. This surfaced a real design decision — is `metadata.reason` reserved or free-form? — resolved **KEEP R3 (reserved)**: QA reshaped `030(1a)` to a valid basis_adjust and switched `030(8a)`/`031(3c)` off the reserved `reason` key → `metadata.note`, and the Architect added the reserved-keys registry. Full suite then **517 PASS** (up from 499 — the 18 previously-aborted assertions now run). The collision was a live demonstration of exactly the poisoning vector R3 guards. Backend clean-apply `001`→`034` under PG17.

**M3-basis (SELF-296) COMPLETE. NEXT in the GL sequence: M4-GL (SELF-297)** — `fn_gl_entries`, the book/market GL/trial-balance INVOKER read helper: the LAST GL migration, consuming everything deferred (M2's `Σ=0`-at-close, M3-basis's roll-forward/accumulated-depreciation/recapture, the journal-grouping write-path, split reads, imputed contras, Suspense). ### v1.99 — 2026-07-23

**Phase 6 — settle-before-import conventions RATIFIED + ADR-031 Amendment 1 LANDED (PR #211, merge `68bd07c`).** Closes the design arc for the double-entry GL: the four conventions ADR-031 Decision 9 flagged as a "settle-before-the-incumbent-import one-way-door" (category→class map, event vocabulary, grouping rule, basis semantics) are now F/CTO-ratified and formalized as an in-place amendment to ADR-031.

**The ratified conventions.** 5-class cashflow enum **`Revenue · Expense · Transfer · Equity · Trade`** (Income→Revenue; Expenses→Expense; Distribution demoted to `Equity::Distribution` symmetrical with `Equity::Contribution`; **Trade** added as a top-level Cat with `BTO/STC/STO/BTC` open/close sub-cats — the designation lives *in* the Cat, superseding the earlier NULL-cat + separate-`open_close`-column partition). **`Transfer ⟺ tax-neutral`** (`Cash` / `Asset`-in-kind moves only; a purchase/sale is a `Trade`, never a Transfer). **Lean event vocabulary `{standard, acct_setup, basis_adjust, corp_action}`** — buy/sell derived from `sign(quantity)`, no explicit security-movement types. **`corp_action`** = splits (qty restatement, no GL) / swaps / ticker-renames — **always a dated transaction, never a registry rename** (a rename would retroactively falsify as-of-date holdings). **Shorts** stored as a negative-quantity holding, presented as a derived `Securities Sold Short` liability. **Naming:** `journal_id` (was `group_id`), `metadata` jsonb (was a `reason` text column). Reclassification-history **deferred to V2**.

**Security re-confirmed GREEN-with-conditions (no veto).** Sec found the Cat-driven `Trade` refinement actually *tightens* the partition (one mutable class source per row on both sides). Two binding conditions attach to the M1-evt/M2 joint-review gates: **(A)** the two Trade constraints (consistency `security_id ⟺ Cat=Trade`; sign-alignment `BTO/BTC⟹qty>0`, `STC/STO⟹qty<0`) are **cross-table**, so realized as **`BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE` overlay triggers** (not single-table CHECKs) — the UPDATE-path re-validation is load-bearing; INVOKER, DEFINER allowlist stays 3, Decision-3-neutral. **(B)** the reclassification-history deferral is valid **only if the monthly-report snapshot ships in V1 as a genuine immutable freeze** (audit-class, not a re-derivable view) — **if the `C+` snapshot migration slips to V2, reclass-history must reopen.**

**Verification.** Brief-drift-catch CLEAN (ratified record + ADR-031 + Sec conditions verified verbatim). Ledgers flat: **§10 3 · SECURITY DEFINER allowlist 3.** No migrations built — decision record only; M1→M4-GL (+ M-hier deferred) each carry their joint-review gates + the two Sec conditions when authored. Design records: `temp/settle-before-import-conventions.md` + `temp/double-entry-transaction-column-map.md` + `temp/double-entry-design-v2.md`. **NEXT:** start the double-entry migrations (M1 Category-as-class = smallest un-gated first step) or the V1 feature clusters (§2.1.2+ net-worth). The **monthly-report snapshot is now load-bearing** for the audit story (Sec Condition B).

### v1.98 — 2026-07-23

**Phase 6 — ADR-031 (event-sourced double-entry general ledger) + ADR-025 Amendment 1 LANDED (F/CTO-ratified 2026-07-23, PR #209, merge `bb76894`).** Formalizes the design decision for a genuine double-entry GL as a **derived layer** over the immutable `account_trans` ledger — no rewrite of the ledger, and **no stored `journal_entry`/`journal_line` structure** (classic Approach B rejected; the earlier "C→B graduation ladder" retired after a stress-test that couldn't break "B never needed" within personal-app scope).

**ADR-031 (9 decisions).** (1) Facts-vs-interpretation event-sourcing — `account_trans` immutability is an audit control over raw FACTS, not a freeze on classification; interpretation (category, open/close, lot-match) lives on the mutable overlay; economic adjustments (RoC, MTM, depreciation) are new dated transactions, not edits. (2) Three buckets (real accounts + imputed flow-contras + Suspense) + manual A/R/A/P accounts + a deferred hierarchical chart-of-accounts escape hatch (`M-hier`). (3) Category-as-class — the top-level cashflow Category *is* the accounting class via a domain-conditional CHECK; **no `flow_class`, no `normal_balance` column**. (4) Dual **book/market** valuation + `basis_adjust` events (magnitude on `cost_basis`, not `amount`; `reason` subtype; accumulated depreciation derived; recapture survives) + an Unrealized-Gains equity line (`net worth = book equity + unrealized gains`; **book-value-primary** F/CTO quick-lock). (5) GL engine = a `SECURITY INVOKER` `fn_gl_entries` helper; N-legged entries via `journal_group_id` grouping of real rows (not journal-lines) + a virtual scratch/Suspense counter-leg; `Σ=0` enforced at group-close (feed legs arrive async → header-comes-after-the-lines). (6) Approach B rejected. (7) Security **conditional-GREEN (no veto)** — binding conditions on M1-evt/M2. (8) Two new Decision-3 instances forward-flagged. (9) Migration plan + the settle-before-import one-way-door.

**ADR-025 Amendment 1.** `transaction_type` immutability scoped to FACTS, not classification, with six preserved commitments (`transaction_type` stays frozen-per-row on the ledger; TEXT+CHECK; fact-level-only vocabulary; settle-before-import; reverse-and-replace as the sole fact-correction path; DEFINER 3 / §10 3 unchanged). In-place amendment; ADR-025 Components 1–3 otherwise unchanged; Status line updated.

**Security posture (2026-07-23).** Both Sec-gated reconciliations conditional-GREEN, no veto: the event-sourcing reframe is audit-sound (the `004` triple-fence never protected interpretation). Binding conditions carried into ADR-031 Decision 7 — chiefly a **strictly per-tenant scratch/Suspense (shared/global = standing VETO)**, **append-only reclassification history** (its own immutability fence, not jsonb), and the **matched-tenant `journal_group` leg fence** (Decision-3 `#12`). Joint-review-mandatory at M1-evt + M2.

**Ledgers flat: §10 stays 3 · DEFINER allowlist stays 3** (every new function/fence INVOKER); Decision-3 family 11 labeled / 9 DDL-realized + **2 forward-flagged** (`journal_group_id` `#12` + lot-match FK). **No migrations built** — this is the decision record; migrations M1→M4-GL (+ M-hier deferred) each carry their joint-review gates when authored. Brief-drift-catch CLEAN (ADR-025 verbatim, the `#5` attribution, the 3-component count, and all ledger counts verified). Design of record: `temp/double-entry-design-v2.md`. **NEXT:** the V1 feature clusters (§2.1.2+ net-worth …) remain the active build track; the double-entry GL is a formalized future V1.x track gated on the settle-before-import conventions.

### v1.96 — 2026-07-22

Auth-3b is now COMPLETE end-to-end.** A user can enroll TOTP → be stepped up at login → have the DB enforce aal2 on the direct API → self-recover a lost authenticator with a rate-limited, scrypt-hashed backup code.

**`027 mfa_recovery_attempt`** (PR #204) — per-user append-only attempt log backing the 5-failures/hr/user lockout; service_role-only (RLS + zero authenticated policy/grant → default-deny both layers), APPEND-ONLY (SELECT+INSERT, no UPDATE/DELETE — a compromised service_role can't erase attempts to reset the gate). pgTAP plan(8); Sec GREEN.

**The recovery app** (PR #205) — the **4th RT-26 `service_role` surface**, the first *actual* `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` usage in `api/src`:
- **`supabase-admin.ts`** — the sole key home (factory pattern; callers hold no key). **ADR-016 Decision 3** amendment + `rt26-allowlist.txt` +1 (allowlist 3→4, intra-instance; **§10 stays 3**; Sec-consult per the webhook-allowlist annotation convention, satisfied in-PR).
- **`redeemRecoveryCode`** — atomic rate-gate (attempt INSERT-before-count + reject-early-but-log-while-locked + fail-closed on log/count error), constant-time compare over ALL ≤10 candidates (no early-return), atomic check-and-consume (`UPDATE … WHERE code_id=? AND users_id=? AND used_at IS NULL`, reject on 0 rows — double-spend TOCTOU), **downgrade-first** (`mfa_policy→none` via the `026` guard-exempt grant, THEN admin `deleteFactor` 3×-retry — fails toward access-restored, never an aal1 hard-lockout), `users_id` **from the validated session only** (service_role BYPASSRLS → explicit `.eq('users_id',…)` on every query). Enumeration-safe (generic invalid/locked). `issueRecoveryCodes` (10 × 80-bit CSPRNG, display-once, supersede batch) + `recoveryCodeStatus`.
- **`mfa-hash.ts`** — Node built-in **async `crypto.scrypt`** per **ADR-030 Amendment 1** (`N=16384/r=8/p=1/keylen=32`, 16-byte salt/code; versioned self-describing `scrypt$v$N$r$p$salt$hash`; verify re-derives with the STORED salt+params; `timingSafeEqual` over fixed-length digests, malformed→false-not-throw). **`bcryptjs` dropped → zero runtime hashing dependency** (F/CTO chose supply-chain minimization; Sec blessed scrypt as memory-hard/NIST/RFC-7914, cryptographically equivalent-or-better for 80-bit one-time codes).
- **`mfa-notify.ts`** — nodemailer + shared SMTP, deploy-gated no-op-with-log, **fail-soft** (never blocks the security op); INV-1 plain-text.
- **UI** — `/mfa/recover` (aal1-reachable redeem) + `/settings/security` display-once `RecoveryCodesPanel` (copy-all / download-.txt / confirm + `beforeunload` guard; INV-1, no value/attn hue; CSP-safe compiled handlers) + aal2-gated regenerate + unused-code count.

**Verification.** Sec hard-gate 🟢 **GREEN** — all 9 conditions on the 4th-RT-26 veto surface (factory sole-key-home, tenant-binding-from-session, scrypt-to-spec, atomic rate-gate + consume, downgrade-first, notify-fail-soft, display-once, enumeration-safe + aal2-regenerate); 3 non-blocking robustness NOTES (N-A deleteFactor-permanent-fail UX, N-B scrypt-v2 version-field, N-C non-sensitive count-timing). `svelte-check` 0 · `vitest` **213** · `vite build` clean · **RT-26 fence exit 0** · live smoke **10/10** (real scrypt + GoTrue). ### v1.95 — 2026-07-22

**Phase 6 — Auth-3b Slice 2a: the recovery-code store SHIPPED (SELF-291, PR #202, merge `1dbabfb`).** The DB foundation for self-service TOTP recovery. Migration `026` lands the store + the column-scoped `service_role` downgrade grant; the actual `/mfa/recover` endpoint + issuance/redemption + UI are Slice 2b (where the 4th RT-26 surface + ADR-016 amendment land).

**`pfin.mfa_recovery_code`.** Per-user store of app-side **bcrypt/argon2-hashed** one-time backup codes (`code_id` PK, `users_id` FK→auth.users ON DELETE CASCADE, `code_hash`, `batch_id`, `used_at` NULL=unspent, `created_at`; partial index `(users_id) WHERE used_at IS NULL`). **service_role-only:** RLS enabled + **ZERO authenticated policy** + **ZERO authenticated/anon grant** → default-deny at *both* the ACL and RLS layers, so hashes never reach the direct PostgREST API (the `linked_source_sync_audit` pattern). `service_role` SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE (redeem reads+consumes, issue inserts; no DELETE — regenerate supersedes via `used_at`, lifecycle via the auth.users cascade).

**The recovery downgrade grant.** `grant select (users_id), update (mfa_policy) on pfin.user_settings to service_role` — column-scoped least-privilege: `service_role` reads *only* the tenant anchor (already visible via `auth.users`) and writes *only* `mfa_policy`. This lets the Slice-2b endpoint perform the guard-exempt `mfa_policy→none` downgrade at recovery (the MB-1 trigger exempts `service_role`). The `select(users_id)` is mechanically required for the `UPDATE … WHERE users_id=$1` (Sec-approved as still minimal — same empirically-forced-but-minimal shape as the MB-1 tier-scoping).

**ADR-030 — the Slice-2 recovery posture.** `service_role` is **forced by construction** — three independent forcings, each sufficient: (1) the MB-1 guard blocks the aal1 `mfa_policy` downgrade; (2) **CV-R1 = BLOCKED** (empirically driven: an aal1 session cannot `mfa.unenroll()` a verified factor — GoTrue returns `422 insufficient_aal`; only `service_role` admin `deleteFactor` works, and removal is *required* or GoTrue keeps `nextLevel=aal2` and the Slice-1 guard still blocks); (3) the hash table must stay off the direct API. So conditional-lock #4 resolves to `service_role` — not because "CV-R1 failed" but because no design escapes it (the DEFINER-RPC alternative still needs `service_role` for the GoTrue leg *and* adds a DEFINER entry — strictly worse; rejected, along with a Sec-vetoable `auth.mfa_factors`-mutation option). Recovery mints **no aal2** (remove dead factor + downgrade → aal1-sufficient → re-enroll). The 4th RT-26 surface + ADR-016 amendment are explicitly deferred to Slice 2b.

**Verification.** Sec joint-review 🟢 **GREEN** (least-privilege grant surface; hashes fenced both layers; §10 framing cross-checked against ADR-016 D1 + Decision 4 verbatim — a `service_role` DB-ACL grant is not an RT-26 code-layer move, same as `008`) + the default-deny pgTAP battery **signed off** (`026` plan(13), new `_rls.stmt_denied_as` verb; full suite **376 tests / 24 files PASS**; non-vacuous — authenticated/anon denied both layers, service_role operability, the `used_at IS NULL` consumption arbiter, the column-scoped grant pinned). All CI green. No functions authored. Ledgers flat: **DEFINER 3 · §10 3 · Decision-3 unchanged.** Migrations `001`–`026` live.

**Remaining on SELF-291** (In Progress): **Slice 2b** — the `/mfa/recover` endpoint (verify→consume→admin `deleteFactor`→downgrade→notify→re-enroll) + display-once issuance + recovery UI + the **ADR-016 amendment (4th RT-26 surface)** + the `rt26-allowlist.txt` update; Sec gates the atomic check-and-consume (single-statement + rows-affected), hash algo/cost + constant-time compare, rate-limit, and notify-on-MFA-change. Plus **C2** (SECURITY §4.5 RT-class posture entry, Sec co-authored, before TOTP GA) + **C3** (migration-checklist note). Detail: PR #202.

### v1.94 — 2026-07-22

**Phase 6 — Auth-3b Slice 1 COMPLETE: the app flow (TOTP enrollment + step-up + fail-closed guard) SHIPPED (SELF-291, PR #200, merge `f7b96c8`).** The app layer on top of the merged `025` DB backstop (v1.93). With enforcement (the DB demands aal2 on the direct API) now paired with a usable enrollment/step-up UI, **opt-in TOTP works end-to-end.**

**Server.** `/settings/security` — the MFA hub: `enrollStart` (`mfa.enroll` → QR + secret) / `enrollVerify` (`challenge`+`verify`; on success sets `mfa_policy='totp'` via a **separate N1-safe UPDATE of only that column, and only after a verified factor exists** — factor-before-policy, so the DB backstop's `totp` never outruns a real factor) / `disable` (aal2-gated; the `025` MB-1 guard rejects an aal1 downgrade at the DB — 42501 → `fail(403)`, factor untouched). `/mfa/step-up` — `getAuthenticatorAssuranceLevel()` → `challenge`+`verify` → aal2 → same-site-guarded redirect. **`requireStepUp` (fail-CLOSED, N3)** — a new helper distinct from the fail-soft `getMfaPolicy` reporter; keys off **GoTrue AAL** as the reconciled source of truth for "a verified factor exists" (**N2**: catches a factor even with a stale `mfa_policy='none'`; N2b lockout prevented by the write-ordering), and any error/indeterminate AAL ⇒ step-up. Sequenced in the `hooks.server.ts` chokepoint as `authHandle → mfaHandle → cspHandle`; **GET-page-nav scope** (POSTs rely on the `025` DB backstop, which clauses writes too, + each action's own `safeGetSession` guard; the exempt-prefix set `/login /signup /auth /mfa` is abuse-resistant — `startsWith(p + '/')` so `/mfa-evil` doesn't match). Anon+RLS only, no service_role.

**UI.** `settings/security` + `mfa/step-up` `.svelte` on Backend's contract; a client Zod mirror (message-identical to the server, Lock 14); the QR rendered via `{@html}` of the trusted, script-free GoTrue-generated SVG — safe under the app-global **nonce-only CSP** (`script-src ['self']` + `mode:'nonce'`, no `unsafe-inline`; avoids a data-URI `<img>` + `img-src data:` widening); `TextField` gains optional native-validation attr passthrough (backward-compatible); plain progressive-enhancement forms (matches `/login` + `/signup`).

**Verification.** Sec joint-review 🟢 **unconditional GREEN** (C-APP1 CSP posture confirmed; the fail-closed guard, N1/N2 reconciliation + write-ordering, the GET-only scope opening no protected-data path, no service_role, and the open-redirect + mass-assignment fences all verified in source). `svelte-check` 0 · `vitest` **181** · `vite build` clean · Backend live smoke **13/13** vs GoTrue v2.189 (enroll→aal2; **totp@aal1 → guard-redirect AND DB-backstop 0 rows AND MB-1 42501**; none-user unaffected) · all CI green. No new migration/DB-fn/service_role (consumes `025`). Ledgers flat: **DEFINER 3 · §10 3 · Decision-3 unchanged.**

**Slice-1 close-out.** MB-1 (the backstop self-defeat) is closed at the DB layer (`025`); this app layer is defense-in-depth over it. Two non-blocking Sec NOTEs: the disable partial-failure window fails in the *safer* access-restored direction (never toward an aal1 lockout); the client Zod mirror is UX-only.

### v1.93 — 2026-07-22

**Phase 6 — Auth-3b Slice-1 (DB enforcement layer): the aal2 step-up backstop + MB-1 guard SHIPPED (SELF-291, PR #198, merge `fa8900c`).** Migration `025` makes opt-in TOTP a real, DB-enforced guarantee — the increment Sec's **C2 hard gate** required. `pfin` is directly reachable via PostgREST + the public anon key (C6); base RLS checks `users_id = auth.uid()` but **not** `aal`, so an `aal1` session (password entered, TOTP not completed) could read/write a totp-user's financial rows through the direct data API with app-layer step-up enforcing nothing. `025` closes that at the only layer that can — the DB.

**The backstop.** A COALESCE-guarded inline clause — `coalesce((select mfa_policy from pfin.user_settings where users_id=auth.uid()),'none') not in ('totp','passkey') or (auth.jwt()->>'aal')='aal2'` — ANDed into **14 tenant tables / 31 policies** (reads + writes). Per-user-conditional, **never blanket** (a `none`/missing-row user is never locked out — the AC's literal clause had a NULL-lockout bug for lazily-provisioned rows, fixed by COALESCE, empirically proven). Tenant isolation (`users_id=auth.uid()`) is preserved **byte-faithfully** across all 31 policies (Sec diffed every one against its authoring migration) and ANDed with — never replaced by — the aal clause. **Inline, not a helper**, by an empirical InitPlan single-eval finding: `set search_path=''` (mandatory function discipline) disables SQL-function inlining, so a helper evaluates per-row while the inline clause evaluates once-per-statement — and it keeps the DEFINER allowlist at 3 by construction (no function authored for the clause).

**MB-1 — the self-defeat, caught + closed.** Sec's adversarial review found the backstop's own control variable (`mfa_policy`) was downgradable at aal1 via the direct API (an attacker `PATCH mfa_policy→'none'` manufactures the fail-open condition and defeats the clause — the same C2 class the migration exists to close). F/CTO ratified **Option A**: a `BEFORE UPDATE` trigger (`fn_user_settings_block_mfa_downgrade`, SECURITY INVOKER) blocking the `authenticated` tier from lowering `mfa_policy` out of `{totp,passkey}` unless the session is aal2. Tier-scoped to `authenticated` (a considered refinement, Sec-confirmed non-spoofable: PostgREST derives the role from the signed JWT claim) so `service_role` recovery + `postgres` admin stay un-gated — consistent-by-construction with the Slice-2 recovery invariants. INVOKER + reads only OLD/NEW/`current_user`/`auth.jwt()` → no table read → no recursion; allowlist stays 3.

**Domain tighten (F/CTO-caught).** A sharp F/CTO question surfaced that the totp-vs-passkey distinction is unused for V1 enforcement (aal2 is factor-agnostic) and that `'passkey'`'s legality was the *sole* enabler of an (inert, self-healing) aal1 lateral flip. Tightened the CHECK to `('none','totp')`; `'passkey'` deferred to Auth-6/SELF-289 (additive re-add, ADR-022). The lateral flip is now a schema-level `23514` rejection — the concern vanishes at the schema layer. The backstop clause + guard keep referencing `{totp,passkey}` internally so re-adding passkey needs zero enforcement-machinery churn.

**ADR-029** (Decisions 1–7) records the posture: the per-user-conditional never-blanket clause, the inline-vs-helper crux, reads+writes scope, COALESCE null-safety, the deliberate DB-fails-open / app-fails-closed layer asymmetry, the MB-1 guard, and the domain tighten. The **Slice-2 recovery invariants** are locked as design constraints: a downgrade/factor-removal is permitted when EITHER the session is aal2 (self-service, no backup code) OR a verified one-time backup code is presented (server-side) — never bare aal1; plus notify-on-every-MFA-change; the backup-code path pre-loads conditional-lock #4 toward the `service_role` fallback, with CV-R1 open only for that path.

**Verification.** Sec joint-review **GREEN** (MB-1 BLOCK cleared; C1 pgTAP battery signed off — non-vacuous blocks [every aal1-zero paired with an aal2-rows-visible], isolation-⟂-MFA proven at both aal levels, false-green-proof exact-message asserts per the 004 lesson). pgTAP `025` plan(29) + `024` reconcile; **full suite 363 tests / 23 files PASS**; all CI green (build, live-DB lane, all fences). Ledgers flat: **DEFINER 3 · §10 3 (RT-22/RT-26/RT-27) · Decision-3 unchanged** — §10 3-axis CLEAN streak extends. Migrations `001`–`025` live. Also caught + corrected a stale "§10 ledger 2" refrain that had propagated through the memo/spec/briefs (canonical is 3 since RT-27 was catalogued 2026-07-19).

**Process note.** Two F/CTO questions materially improved the migration mid-review — the Slice-2 recovery-invariant correction (aal2 self-service must not be routed through backup-code-only) and deleting the speculative `passkey` value — and Sec's adversarial pass caught MB-1 before it shipped. Built via role-separated teams (architect → security-reviewer ‖ qa), design-first, with team-lead cross-checks at each brief boundary.

**Remaining on SELF-291** (In Progress): the Slice-1 **app flow** — enrollment + the fail-closed `requireStepUp` guard (`hooks.server.ts`) + N2/N3 reconciliation (separate PR, its own Sec joint-review gate; §7 ordering satisfied — enforcement landed first); **C2** (SECURITY §4.5 RT-class posture entry, Sec co-authored, before TOTP GA) + **C3** (migration-checklist note); and **Slice 2** — recovery codes (`026` + redemption + CV-R1) under the ratified invariants. Detail: PR #198.

### v1.86 — 2026-07-20

**Phase 6 — SELF-280 (connect-wave follow-up #16): `safeGetSession` unvalidated-session hardening (PR #181, merge `2a5b18c`).** Hardens the [ADR-015](DECISIONS.md#adr-015) D1 single auth chokepoint (`api/src/hooks.server.ts`). `safeGetSession` validated the JWT via `getUser()` but returned the raw `getSession()` session, whose `.user` came from the **unverified (spoofable) cookie**. It now normalizes the returned `session.user` to the `getUser()`-server-validated user — `return { session: { ...session, user }, user }` — so any read of `session.user` is validated-by-construction. Latent (all 9 call sites read only `{ user }`) but a footgun on the app's sole auth chokepoint.

**Also fail-closed-strengthening.** `if (error)` → `if (error || !user)` — a type-narrow that ALSO closes a prior `{ session, user: null }` gap on the no-error/null-user path; `authHandle` exported for the test (non-behavioral, mirrors the `cspHandle` precedent). **Sec joint-review GREEN** (auth surface, no conditions — fail-closed preserved and strengthened). **Ledger UNCHANGED: RT-26 allowlist untouched** (anon+RLS path only; `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` never referenced) · **§10 stays 3 · DEFINER 3 · Decision-3 8** · no DB change / no SECURITY DEFINER. 3 new `hooks.server.test.ts` cases (spoofed-≠-validated ⇒ validated user in both `user` + `session.user`; + no-session / getUser-error ⇒ {null,null}); 63 api tests pass, `svelte-check` 0 errors, 10/10 CI. Detail: [PR #181](https://github.com/richmosko/mosko-fintech/pull/181).

BackendApi re-applied + committed (`8df3fad`), the tree verified whole + green. **One-way-door RATIFIED — worker-owns-exchange (F/CTO, with Architect + Sec both endorsing).** `api/src` will be a thin browser-facing relay passing ONLY the short-lived (~30-min, single-use) Plaid `public_token`; the WORKER does the `public_token`→`access_token` exchange + `vault.create_secret` + `INSERT linked_source`. The `access_token` + Plaid client secret + the service_role vault-write all stay in the worker (off the browser edge, off the RT-26 allowlist). Sec assessed it security-superior on every axis (credential-surface minimization, RT-26 stays narrow, smaller blast radius, clean SoD — §4.1 owns the browser edge, SC-3/Decision-1 owns the credential). Rejected app-owns-exchange (would inject the Plaid secret + a service_role vault-write into the browser-facing tier). Pinned in **ADR-027 (s)–(v)** (worker-owns split; `ownerUserId` server-session-derived ADR-LOCK per SC3-C1; inline service_role SQL no-DEFINER; dev-CLI-first sandbox-gated).

**What landed (`workers/provider-sync/`, 784 insertions / 7 files).** `connect(setup)` — Plaid `/item/public_token/exchange` + `/accounts/get`, then ONE atomic `withServiceRole` tx: `vault.create_secret` → `INSERT pfin.linked_source(credential_secret_id, users_id=ownerUserId, …)`; **re-admission** (dedup `(provider, external_connection_id)`) reuses the SAME handle via `vault.update_secret` (no second-secret orphan) + the **SC3-C8 tenant-match guard** (`if found.users_id !== ownerUserId → throw`, fail-closed, before any mutation); returns account refs only (NO `pfin.account` write — deferred to the account-mapping slice, keeps the shipped #6 fence + Decision-3 untouched). `revoke(ref)` — decrypt-read → Plaid `/item/remove` → **tenant-scoped** DELETE (both statements filter `users_id`, deviation #1 — Sec-blessed defense-in-depth); **abort-on-revoke-failure** (never orphan a live Item), `ITEM_NOT_FOUND`→proceed (idempotent). A **sandbox-gated dev CLI** (`src/cli/admit.ts`, enforces `PLAID_ENV=sandbox`; never the production path).

**Sec conditions (all cleared or forward-tracked).** SC3-C2 (CLI sandbox-gate), C3 (uuid tenant-guard before any DB write + `.strict()` mass-assignment reject), C4 (raw `access_token` never logged/returned/persisted-outside-Vault — bound as query param, whole-axios-error discard), C5 (revoke abort atomicity), C8 (re-admission tenant-match parity) — all verified in code. Deferred: **C1** (app-relay `ownerUserId`-from-server-session, ADR-locked, binds at SELF-212) + **C6** (the app→worker handoff SC-review, SELF-212).
